1: # Hostile mathematics audit: k6 rule delta (L9–L12, erratum v1, CERT-FORMAT v1)
2: 
3: **Scope.** I re-derived everything by hand from the inlined texts. No code, run, count or survivor list was supplied, and none was audited (that includes the 263 orbits at SOURCE01:5). Nothing here says any pattern dies. No k6 theorem, survivor claim or novelty is asserted. This is one Claude-family review.
4: 
5: **Bottom line.** I found no FATAL defect. Each rule in the delta is a valid necessary condition for every genuine Core5(6) configuration in the minimal frame. That covers all admissible τ, all pairwise-coprime r (including r_i = 1, arbitrary prime powers and primes ≥ 7), every coincident or absent marker, every q and all seven E5 placements. Two qualifications:
6: - L11 has one MAJOR proof gap. The claim is true, and the repair is given below.

9: | Component | Verdict |

18: ## 1. L9 (SOURCE00:8–34; SOURCE02:28–66)
19: 
20: **Verified.**
21: - **(a) Left cosets of H.**
22:   - The piece is g_lU_l ∩ C = y_l(U_l∩H).
23:   - Translating by z⁻¹ gives left cosets in H of index λ_l = 5r_lt_l/5 = r_lt_l.
24:   - Disjointness passes to every subset T.
25: - **(b) Right cosets of U_v.**
26:   - The piece is A_jw_j, with A_j = U_v ∩ w_jU_jw_j⁻¹.
27:   - [U_v:A_j] = |U_vw_jU_j|/|U_j| = d_j = q_jr_j, independent of w.
28:   - c_j = w_jw_0⁻¹ lies in U_v, and inversion gives disjoint left cosets c_j⁻¹A_j.
29: - **Which Sun is used.**
30:   - In U_v: L0.6, Zhu, and the accepted Sun(5), since |T| ≤ |N| = 5.
31:   - Sun(6) appears only in (a), and only when all six vertices meet one coset.
32: - **Exactness of maxgcd and fgcd.**
33:   - Common primes of q_jr_j and q_kr_k lie in {2,3}, since q ≤ 4.
34:   - Common primes of r_lt_l lie in {2,3,5}, since t ≤ 5.
35:   - A prime ≥ 7, or a second marked cofactor, never contributes.
36:   - For class-2 E2, E3 and E5, the minimum saturates at the partner's t- or q-part: v₂ ≤ 2 ≤ v₂(r), and v₃, v₅ ≤ 1 ≤ v_p(r). Class 1 is exact.
37:   - So maxgcd and fgcd equal the true gcd for every instance. The test is upward-closed in each gcd anyway.
38: - **Family relation.**
39:   - The hereditary family lies inside the v1 family (take T = the pairs, then T = S), and it is downward closed.
40:   - Every removed column and every extra rejection is justified pointwise.
41:   - "Maximal sets suffice" still holds.
42: 
43: **Findings.**
44: - **MINOR SOURCE00:10, 23.** The statement imports the L5 setting (H ≤ G), but step 3 relies on "H is proper".
45:   - With H = G, the case |T| = 6 is Sun(6) for G itself, which is circular.
46:   - Fix: put "H < G whenever |T| = 6" into the statement. E4 (SOURCE03:30–32) amends only v1 L5.
47:   - Also SOURCE02:31: "|S| = 6 is used" should read "|T| = 6".
48: - **MINOR SOURCE02:62–66.** Exactness is proved only for setting (b). Add the FH5 case explicitly, citing SOURCE03:36–38, including p = 5: v₅(t) ≤ 1 ≤ v₅(r_E5).
49: 
50: ## 2. L10 XE (SOURCE01:7–19; SOURCE02:68–83)
51: 
52: Every step checks:
53: - X·U_j = X·U_k = X, so XW = X.
54: - U_v ⊆ X, so X = U_vW.
55: - α_vj ≤ 4, so W ≠ G.
56: - U_j, U_k ≤ W, so [G:W] divides gcd(5r_j, 5r_k) = 5.
57: - μ(U_jU_k) ≤ μ(W), so α_jk = 1 and U_jU_k = W.
58: - The product formula gives [U_v:U_v∩W] = α_vj, and hence also α_vj = α_vk.
59: 
60: Equal subgroups (r = 1) are a consistent special case. **No findings.**
61: 
62: Optional, sound strengthening: in P and RPLP, when x_j = x_k and α_jk ≥ 2, XE bars equality. So hi may drop by u even when both K2 inequalities hold.
63: 
64: ## 3. L11 N2-6 (SOURCE01:21–46; SOURCE02:85–119)
65: 
66: **Verified.**
67: - **Step 1.**
68:   - (T1) on edge jk, which is opposite v, gives α_jk | r_vτ_vjk.
69:   - gcd(r_v, α_jk) = 1, so τ_vjk ≥ α_jk and μ(Z_jk) ≥ 1/5.
70:   - Granularity (L0.4/L0.5): gcd(5r_v, 5r_jr_kα_jk) = 5·gcd(r_v, α_jk) = 5.
71:   - XE is genuinely needed: K2 permits X_j ⊆ X_k when α_jk = 2, since 2 + 2 ≤ 4.
72:   - Hence μ(X_j∩X_k) = 1/5 = μ(Z_jk).
73: - **Step 2.**
74:   - Q is a multiple of 5r_jr_k·lcm(r_lτ_jkl, r_vτ_vjk).
75:   - Given gcd(r_v, τ_jkl) = 1, this gives Q ≥ r_v[G:W] and μ(U_vW) ≥ 1/5.
76: - **Step 3.**
77:   - Pairs sharing a vertex share a triple, and disjoint pairs chain through a third pair.
78:   - The count is 1/5 + 5·(1/5) = 6/5 > 1.
79: 
80: **MAJOR (proof gap; the claim is true): SOURCE01:42; SOURCE02:93, 109, 119.**
81: - "τ involves only the primes 2 and 3" is asserted, not proved.
82: - (T1) and (T2) alone give only τ_min | τ ≤ τ_max ≤ 16. A priori that admits 5, 7, 10 and 14.
83: - The claim is load-bearing exactly where the certificate carries no data: v = E5, and primes ≥ 7 in r_v.
84: 
85: Repair, which I verified:
86: - For j, k, l ≠ v, all three α lie in {2,4}, and at most one edge is opposite E2. So τ_min ∈ {2,4}.
87: - τ_max is 4, 8 or 16 when at most one, exactly two, or all three edges equal 4.
88: - With two or more 4-edges, at least one is not opposite E2, so τ_min = 4.
89: - Hence the admissible τ_jkl form one of the sets {2,4}, {4}, {4,8} or {4,8,12,16}.
90: 
91: Consequences:
92: - gcd(r_v, τ_jkl) = 1 for every odd r_v with 3 ∤ r_v. That includes r_v = 1, E5, and arbitrary primes or prime powers.
93: - 3 | τ_jkl happens only as τ = 12 in a triangle whose three edges all equal 4.
94: - Insert this table at SOURCE02:93.
95: - Remark: a longer check shows that every admissible τ in all of Core5(6) lies in {1,2,3,4,6,8,9,12,16}. This is not needed here.
96: 
97: **MINOR (wrong direction, but unreachable): SOURCE01:27; SOURCE02:93; SOURCE04:162.**
98: - If 3 | τ_min, then 3 divides every admissible τ, so (iv) fails for v = E3. The first alternative, "3 | τ_min", points the wrong way.
99: - SOURCE01:27's "whenever 3 ∤ τ_min" exempts exactly that bad case.
100: - Example outside (iii): a (3,3,3) triangle has τ_min = 3 and τ ∈ {3,6,9}.
101: - Under (iii), τ_min | 4, so the disjunct never fires and no invalid N26 is accepted.
102: - Fix: replace it with "τ_max < 3τ_min for every triple". Under (iii) this is equivalent to "no all-4 triangle in N".
103: - Sufficiency holds: if 3 | mτ_min and 3 ∤ τ_min, then m ≥ 3.
104: 
105: ## 4. L12 RPLP (SOURCE01:48–80; SOURCE02:121–174)
106: 
107: **Decomposition.**
108: - X_j, Y_j and D_j are left U_v-invariant, so every right coset lies in X_j or in Y_j.
109: - S_C ⊆ A_C by (R1).
110: - S_C is hereditary-allowed by L9(b), with maxgcd exact.
111: 
112: **Forced containments.**
113: - On a T-consistent leaf, τ_min ≥ α_vwα_wj ≥ τ by (T2) at apex w, and τ ≥ τ_min, so τ is pinned.
114: - (T3) at apex v gives μ(U_v(U_w∩U_j)) = α_vw/5 = μ(X_w). So X_w ⊆ X_j, that is, Y_j ⊆ Y_w.
115: - The column bar has the correct orientation and holds for every admissible τ.
116: 
117: **Constraints.**
118: - E1 and E2 are exact.
119: - In B, u = 1/(5·gcd(r_v, α_jk)) is the exact P2 unit.
120: - lo ≤ μ(X_j∩X_k), because τ_min ≤ τ.
121: - hi ≥ μ(X_j∩X_k). I checked the K2-bar orientation in all three cases.
122: - B depends on r only through the markers. E5 never enters.
123: - TOT ≤ 1 − 1/(5r_v).
124: 
125: **Column completeness.** The only columns omitted are those with:
126: - A = ∅ (these appear in none of E1, E2, B or Σ);
127: - a violated forced containment;
128: - S not allowed;
129: - S ⊄ A.
130: 
131: Each has true mass 0. No realisable type is omitted.
132: 
133: **Duality.**
134: - Weak duality applied directly to the true x gives Σx ≥ Obj ≥ 1 > TOT.
135: - Farkas is needed only for existence. Scaling keeps the right-hand side at 0 ≤ 1.
136: - A pair with lo > hi also gets a valid certificate: put a large equal weight on u_p and l_p.
137: - All arithmetic is rational.
138: 
139: **q.** q_j ranges over [1, 5 − α_vj] by (R2) and (R1). Every q is covered once the per-q clause in §6 is enforced.
140: 
141: **No findings.**
142: 
143: ## 5. Erratum v1 (SOURCE03)
144: 
145: The folding is complete:
146: - Codex MINORs → E1–E5;
147: - Codex MAJOR (controls) → E8;
148: - Claude MINORs → E1, E3, E5–E8.
149: 
150: No engine predicate is silently altered:
151: - E4 adds a hypothesis that FH5 satisfies.
152: - E6 spells out the minimum search space that soundness already requires.
153: - E5 replaces a false justification without changing any gcd vector.
154: 
155: **Findings.**
156: - **MINOR SOURCE03:15.** The frozen contract binds proof-v3 (SOURCE07:33, 46, 47).
157:   - Rebinding to the v4 verdict is licensed by SOURCE11:33.
158:   - The gate wording must say that the contract's v3 dependence is discharged through that verdict; an erratum cannot amend a frozen contract.
159: - **MINOR SOURCE03:27.** State which allowed family a dual must respect: the v1 family for engine-v1 duals, the hereditary family (SOURCE00:32) for engines v2/v3. Both are sound.
160: - **MINOR (optional) SOURCE03:17.** The "any five cosets" remark also closes the p = 3 branch.
161: - **Cosmetic.** The erratum item "E5" collides with the marker name E5.
162: 
163: ## 6. CERT-FORMAT v1 (SOURCE04)
164: 
165: **Each check, if it passes, implies its rule's conclusion.**
166: - **K.** A link at τ_min pins τ, and Σ ≥ 5 contradicts the bound Σ ≤ 4.
167: - **Ppair/PB.** u, lo, hi and the bars are as in §4. The extra precondition lo ≤ hi is harmless.
168: - **RLP.** Weak duality over all hereditary-allowed S.
169: - **FH5.**
170:   - (i) is F3 from every vertex with t = 1. (ii) is L9(a) with exact fgcd.
171:   - Only a's coset is fixed. b, the inside vertices and the forced vertices range fully.
172:   - All seven E5 placements, including coincident ones, are refuted.
173: - **N26.** L11 plus the §3 repair.
174: - **RPLP.** (★) plus the objective, with the columns rebuilt by the checker.
175: 
176: **Universe.**
177: - The 8 types are complete. Their stabilisers are S6 (once), S5 (five times) and S4 (twice).
178: - T-consistency comes from the exact table.
179: - A certificate for an orbit representative transfers to the whole orbit: every rule is label-equivariant, and the stabilisers fix the markers.
180: 
181: **Clauses a checker must add.** Without these, a literal implementation could accept a bad witness.
182: 
183: 1. **SAT for prime 5 (SOURCE04:149–152).** SAT is defined only for 2 and 3 (SOURCE04:98).
184:    - Require SAT₂ ≥ 2, SAT₃ ≥ 1 and SAT₅ ≥ 1.
185:    - Example: e5 = l with t_l = 1 and t_l′ = 5 has a true 5-part of 5. With SAT₅ = 0, fgcd is underestimated and the checker over-rejects, which is unsound.
186: 2. **Recompute V (SOURCE04:136).**
187:    - Reject a witness V that contains any v with α_va ≠ α_vb. Forcing t_v = α_va for such a v can exclude the true assignment.
188:    - A subset of the true V is safe.
189:    - Also check α_ab = 1 and a ≠ b.
190: 3. **Well-formedness.**
191:    - v ∈ 0..5.
192:    - In K and PB, S must be a duplicate-free subset of the vertices other than v. Duplicates inflate Σα_iv or Σ(1 − x_j) and produce degenerate pairs.
193:    - In Ppair, j ≠ k and both lie in N.
194:    - y has length 5 and vec has length 30.
195:    - Rationals are parsed exactly; floats are rejected.
196: 4. **Ordered forced pairs (SOURCE04:174).** (w,j) is ordered, with the product taken at w. Test both orders separately: an unordered implementation over-prunes columns and is unsound.
197: 5. **Per-q coverage (SOURCE04:168–169).**
198:    - Reject unless len(certs) = ∏(5 − α_vj).
199:    - For ["R", key], recompute the key from (q, st) and require equality. Otherwise a dual for an easier q could be cited.
200:    - Reject duplicate keys in rlp_duals.json.
201: 6. **"Should" → "must" (SOURCE04:80).**
202:    - Enumerate the T-consistent leaves of each type independently, pruning only at completed triangles.
203:    - Compute orbits under the stabiliser.
204:    - Require exactly one valid line per orbit, with the type field among the 8 and the representative of that type.
205:    - leafcodes_sha256 is only a cross-check.
206:    - Align SOURCE01:84 (pruned-node count published) with SOURCE04:66 (count informational).
207: 7. **SOURCE04:162.** Delete the "3 | τ_min" disjunct.
208: 8. **SOURCE04:199.** Because FH5 uses Sun(6) inside H < G, what the certificate set actually shows is:
209:    - *no minimal-order Sun(6) counterexample carries a Core5(6) configuration;*
210:    - *with R1(6) (SOURCE06:47), Sun(6) follows, and Core5(6) follows from Sun(6).*
211: 9. **Obligation outside the checker.** E5 is not part of the type. So E5-independence of K, P, RLP, RPLP and N26 is a written obligation. For N26 it rests on the §3 MAJOR repair.
212:
